Players = game:GetService("Players")
ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
Workspace = game:GetService("Workspace")

plr = Players.LocalPlayer

function StartPolarAntiKick()
    inv = Workspace:FindFirstChild(plr.Name.."SpawnedInToys")
    local hrp
    setOwner = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("GrabEvents"):WaitForChild("SetNetworkOwner")
    stickyEvent = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("PlayerEvents"):WaitForChild("StickyPartEvent")
    destroyrem = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("MenuToys"):WaitForChild("DestroyToy")
    canSpawn = plr:WaitForChild("CanSpawnToy")
    local function getHRP()
        if plr.Character and plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
            return plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart
        else
            character = plr.CharacterAdded:Wait()
            return character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        end
    end
    local function CheckForHome()
        local ToyFolder
        if not Workspace.PlotItems.PlayersInPlots:FindFirstChild(plr.Name) then 
            return false
        end
        for _, v in pairs(Workspace.Plots:GetChildren()) do
            for _, b in pairs(v.PlotSign.ThisPlotsOwners:GetChildren()) do
                if b.Value == plr.Name then
                    ToyFolder = Workspace.PlotItems[v.Name]
                end
            end
        end
        if ToyFolder then 
            return true, ToyFolder
        else 
            return false
        end
    end
    local function StickKunai(kunai)
        if not kunai or not kunai:FindFirstChild("StickyPart") then return end
        currentHRP = getHRP()
        if kunai:FindFirstChild("SoundPart") then
            if not kunai["SoundPart"]:FindFirstChild("PartOwner") or kunai["SoundPart"].PartOwner.Value ~= plr.Name then 
                setOwner:FireServer(kunai.SoundPart, kunai.SoundPart.CFrame)
            end
        end
        stickyEvent:FireServer(
            kunai.StickyPart,
            currentHRP:FindFirstChild("FirePlayerPart") or currentHRP:WaitForChild("FirePlayerPart"),
            CFrame.new(0,0,0) * CFrame.Angles(0,math.rad(90),math.rad(90))
        )
        for _, obj in pairs(kunai:GetChildren()) do
            if obj.Name == "Pyramid" then
                obj.CanTouch = false
                obj.CanCollide = false
                obj.CanQuery = false
                obj.Transparency = 0
                high = Instance.new("Highlight")
                high.FillColor = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
                high.Parent = obj
            elseif obj.Name == "Main" then
                obj.CanTouch = false
                obj.CanCollide = false
                obj.CanQuery = false
                obj.Transparency = 0
                high = Instance.new("Highlight")
                high.FillColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                high.Parent = obj
            elseif obj:IsA("BasePart") then
                obj.CanTouch = false
                obj.CanCollide = false
                obj.CanQuery = false
                obj.Transparency = 1
            end
        end
    end
    local function ClearKunai()
        currentInv = Workspace:FindFirstChild(plr.Name.."SpawnedInToys")
        if currentInv and destroyrem then
            for _,v in pairs(currentInv:GetChildren()) do
                if v.Name == "AntiKick" then
                    pcall(function() destroyrem:FireServer(v) end)
                end
            end
        end
    end
    local function SpawnToy(name)
        while not canSpawn.Value do
            canSpawn.Changed:Wait()
        end
        currentHRP = getHRP()
        task.spawn(function()
            ReplicatedStorage.MenuToys.SpawnToyRemoteFunction:InvokeServer(
                name,
                currentHRP.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 12, 20),
                Vector3.new(0,0,0)
            )
        end)
        boolik, house = CheckForHome()
        currentInv = Workspace:FindFirstChild(plr.Name.."SpawnedInToys")
        if boolik then 
            return house:WaitForChild(name, 2)
        elseif not Workspace.PlotItems.PlayersInPlots:FindFirstChild(plr.Name) then 
            return currentInv and currentInv:WaitForChild(name, 2)
        elseif Workspace.PlotItems.PlayersInPlots:FindFirstChild(plr.Name) and not boolik then 
            return nil
        end
    end
    while _G.ShurikenAntiKick do 
        task.wait(0.005)
        if not plr.Character or not plr.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") or plr.Character.Humanoid.Health <= 0 then 
            continue 
        end
        currentInv = Workspace:FindFirstChild(plr.Name.."SpawnedInToys")
        if not currentInv then continue end
        kunai = currentInv:FindFirstChild("NinjaShuriken")
        if Workspace.PlotItems.PlayersInPlots:FindFirstChild(plr.Name) then 
            boolik, house = CheckForHome()
            if boolik and house and Workspace.Plots:FindFirstChild(house.Name) and Workspace.Plots:FindFirstChild(house.Name)["PlotSign"]["ThisPlotsOwners"]:FindFirstChild("Value") and Workspace.Plots:FindFirstChild(house.Name)["PlotSign"]["ThisPlotsOwners"]["Value"]["TimeRemainingNum"].Value > 89 then 
                kunai = SpawnToy("NinjaShuriken")
                if kunai == nil then continue end
                kunai.Name = "AntiKick" 
                StickKunai(kunai)
            end
        end
        if not kunai then
            if Workspace.PlotItems.PlayersInPlots:FindFirstChild(plr.Name) then continue end 
            kunai = SpawnToy("NinjaShuriken")
            if kunai == nil then continue end 
            kunai.Name = "AntiKick"
            if not kunai then continue end 
        end
        repeat
            if kunai and kunai:FindFirstChild("StickyPart") and kunai.StickyPart.CanTouch == true then
                StickKunai(kunai)
                kunai.Name = "AntiKick"
            end
            wait(0.3)
        until not kunai or not _G.ShurikenAntiKick 
            or not kunai:FindFirstChild("StickyPart")
            or kunai.StickyPart.CanTouch == false 
            or not plr.Character or not plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") 
            or not kunai:FindFirstChild("StickyPart") 
            or (plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position - kunai.StickyPart.Position).Magnitude >= 20
        if not kunai or not kunai:FindFirstChild("StickyPart") or not plr.Character or not plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or (plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position - kunai.StickyPart.Position).Magnitude >= 20 then 
            ClearKunai()
        end 
        pcall(function()
            repeat
                wait(0.05)
            until not _G.ShurikenAntiKick or not plr.Character or not plr.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") or not kunai or not kunai:FindFirstChild("StickyPart") or not kunai.StickyPart:FindFirstChild("StickyWeld") or not kunai.StickyPart.StickyWeld.Part1
            if not kunai or not kunai:FindFirstChild("StickyPart") or (plr.Character and plr.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and plr.Character.Humanoid.Health <= 0) or not kunai["StickyPart"]:FindFirstChild("StickyWeld").Part1 then 
                ClearKunai()
            end
        end)
    end
end

ClassicDefense:AddToggle("AntiKick", {
    Text = "Anti Kick [Shuriken]",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(v)
        _G.ShurikenAntiKick = v
        if v then
            task.spawn(StartPolarAntiKick)
        else
            currentInv = Workspace:FindFirstChild(plr.Name.."SpawnedInToys")
            destroyrem = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("MenuToys") and ReplicatedStorage.MenuToys:FindFirstChild("DestroyToy")
            if currentInv and destroyrem then
                for _, item in pairs(currentInv:GetChildren()) do
                    if item.Name == "AntiKick" or item.Name == "NinjaShuriken" then
                        pcall(function() destroyrem:FireServer(item) end)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
})

do
    local Players = game:GetService("Players")
    local RS = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
    local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
    local plr = Players.LocalPlayer
    local AntiKickItemActive = false
    local MyPCLD = nil
    local pcldConn = nil
    local AntiKickToyList2 = {
        ["Japanese Lantern"] = "JapaneseLantern",
        ["Spray Can"]        = "SprayCanWD",
        ["Spooky Candle"]    = "SpookyCandle1",
        ["Clock"]            = "ClockAlarm"
    }
    local DropdownValues = {}
    for shortName, _ in pairs(AntiKickToyList2) do
        table.insert(DropdownValues, shortName)
    end
    table.sort(DropdownValues)
    local SelectedToy = AntiKickToyList2["Clock"] or AntiKickToyList2[DropdownValues[1]]
    ClassicDefense:AddDropdown("AntiKickItemSelect", {
        Values = DropdownValues,
        Default = 4,
        Multi = false,
        Text = "Anti Kick Item",
        Callback = function(Value)
            SelectedToy = AntiKickToyList2[Value]
        end
    })
    local function GetMagnitude(Part1, Part2)
        return (Part1.Position - Part2.Position).Magnitude
    end
    local function FWD(parent, part, timeOffset)
        return parent:FindFirstChild(part) or parent:WaitForChild(part, timeOffset or 1)
    end
    local function CFP(parent, part)
        return parent:FindFirstChild(part) ~= nil  
    end
    local function CheckNetworkOwnerOnPart(Part) 
        local po = Part:FindFirstChild("PartOwner")
        return po and po.Value == plr.Name
    end
    local function sno(part)
        pcall(function()
            local grabEvents = RS:FindFirstChild("GrabEvents")
            local setNetOwner = grabEvents and grabEvents:FindFirstChild("SetNetworkOwner")
            if setNetOwner then
                setNetOwner:FireServer(part, part.CFrame)
            end
        end)
    end
    local function CheckForHome()
        local plotItems = workspace:FindFirstChild("PlotItems")
        local plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")
        if plots and plotItems then
            for i = 1, 5 do 
                local Plot = plots:FindFirstChild("Plot"..i)
                if Plot then
                    local sign = Plot:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")
                    local owners = sign and sign:FindFirstChild("ThisPlotsOwners")
                    if owners then
                        for _,v in pairs(owners:GetChildren()) do 
                            if v.Value == plr.Name then 
                                return plotItems:FindFirstChild("Plot"..i)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        return nil
    end
    local function SpawnToy(ToyName)
        local InPlot = plr:FindFirstChild("InPlot")
        local InOwnedPlot = plr:FindFirstChild("InOwnedPlot")
        local CanSpawnToy = plr:FindFirstChild("CanSpawnToy")
        local inv = workspace:FindFirstChild(plr.Name.."SpawnedInToys")
        if InPlot and InPlot.Value and InOwnedPlot and not InOwnedPlot.Value then 
            InPlot:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Value"):Wait()
        end 
        if CanSpawnToy and not CanSpawnToy.Value then 
            CanSpawnToy:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Value"):Wait()
        end
        local hrp = plr.Character and plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not hrp then return nil end
        local SpawnCF = (MyPCLD or hrp).CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 14, 20)
        local Container = (InOwnedPlot and InOwnedPlot.Value) and CheckForHome() or inv
        if not Container then return nil end
        local spawnedObject = nil
        local connection
        connection = Container.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
            if child.Name == ToyName then
                spawnedObject = child
            end
        end)
        task.spawn(function()
            pcall(function()
                local menuToys = RS:FindFirstChild("MenuToys")
                local spawnRemote = menuToys and menuToys:FindFirstChild("SpawnToyRemoteFunction")
                if spawnRemote then
                    spawnRemote:InvokeServer(ToyName, SpawnCF, Vector3.zero)
                end
            end)
        end)
        local start = tick()
        repeat task.wait() until spawnedObject or (tick() - start) > 2.5
        if connection then connection:Disconnect() end
        return spawnedObject
    end
    local function FindPCLD(hrp)
        if pcldConn then pcldConn:Disconnect() end
        MyPCLD = nil
        pcldConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if MyPCLD or not hrp or not hrp.Parent then 
                if pcldConn then pcldConn:Disconnect() pcldConn = nil end
                return
            end
            for _, v in pairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do 
                if v.Name == "PlayerCharacterLocationDetector" and v:IsA("BasePart") then
                    if GetMagnitude(v, hrp) <= 2 then 
                        MyPCLD = v
                        break
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
    end
    ClassicDefense:AddToggle("AntiKickItem", {
        Text = "Anti Kick [ITEM]",
        Default = false,
        Callback = function(Val)
            AntiKickItemActive = Val 
            if Val then
                task.spawn(function()
                    local Item, SoundPart
                    while AntiKickItemActive and task.wait() do 
                        local char = plr.Character
                        local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        local hum = char and char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
                        local inPlot = plr:FindFirstChild("InPlot")
                        local inv = workspace:FindFirstChild(plr.Name.."SpawnedInToys")
                        local destroyToy = RS:FindFirstChild("MenuToys") and RS.MenuToys:FindFirstChild("DestroyToy")
                        if not hrp or not hum or hum.Health <= 0 or not inv then continue end  
                        if inPlot and inPlot.Value then continue end 
                        if not MyPCLD and not pcldConn then
                            FindPCLD(hrp)
                        end
                        Item = inv:FindFirstChild("AntiKickItem") 
                        SoundPart = Item and Item:FindFirstChild("Hitbox")
                        if not Item or not SoundPart then
                            for _,v in pairs(inv:GetChildren()) do 
                                if v.Name == "AntiKickItem" then 
                                    pcall(function() destroyToy:FireServer(v) end)
                                end
                            end
                            Item = SpawnToy(SelectedToy)
                            if not Item then continue end 
                            SoundPart = Item and FWD(Item, "Hitbox", 0.5)
                            if SoundPart then sno(SoundPart) end
                            for _,v in pairs(Item:GetChildren()) do 
                                if v:IsA("BasePart") then 
                                    v.CanCollide = false 
                                    v.Transparency = 0.9
                                    v.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                                end
                            end
                            Item.Name = "AntiKickItem"
                        end
                        if Item and Item.Parent then
                            for _,v in pairs(inv:GetChildren()) do
                                if v.Name == "AntiKickItem" and v ~= Item then
                                    pcall(function() destroyToy:FireServer(v) end)
                                end
                            end
                        end
                        if SoundPart and not CheckNetworkOwnerOnPart(SoundPart) then 
                            sno(SoundPart)
                        end
                        local targetPart = MyPCLD or hrp:FindFirstChild("FirePlayerPart") or hrp
                        if SoundPart and targetPart then
                            SoundPart.CFrame = targetPart.CFrame
                            SoundPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                            SoundPart.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                        end
                    end
                end)
            else
                if pcldConn then pcldConn:Disconnect() pcldConn = nil end
                MyPCLD = nil
                task.spawn(function()
                    local inv = workspace:FindFirstChild(plr.Name.."SpawnedInToys")
                    local destroyToy = RS:FindFirstChild("MenuToys") and RS.MenuToys:FindFirstChild("DestroyToy")
                    if inv and destroyToy then
                        for _,v in pairs(inv:GetChildren()) do 
                            if v.Name == "AntiKickItem" then 
                                pcall(function() destroyToy:FireServer(v) end)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end)
            end
        end
    })
    plr.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
        if AntiKickItemActive then
            MyPCLD = nil
            local hrp = char:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 5)
            if hrp then FindPCLD(hrp) end
        end
    end)
end

ClassicDefense:AddDivider()

ClassicDefense:AddLabel("Burn & Blobman", true, Color3.fromRGB(255, 150, 50))

ClassicDefense:AddToggle("AntiBurn", {
    Text = "Anti Burn",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(v)
        if v then
            getgenv().AntiBurnCharConn = plr.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(ch)
                if getgenv().AntiBurnConn then getgenv().AntiBurnConn:Disconnect() end
                local hum = ch:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 0.5)
                if not hum then return end
                local fireDebounce = hum:FindFirstChild("FireDebounce")
                if not fireDebounce then return end
                getgenv().AntiBurnConn = fireDebounce.Changed:Connect(function()
                    if fireDebounce.Value == true then
                        local bar = Workspace.Plots.Plot1.Barrier.PlotBarrier
                        local pos = bar.CFrame
                        task.spawn(function()
                            repeat task.wait() bar.CFrame = ch.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame until not fireDebounce.Value
                        end)
                        task.wait(1)
                        fireDebounce.Value = false
                        task.wait()
                        bar.CFrame = pos
                    end
                end)
            end)
            local currentHum = plr.Character and plr.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
            if currentHum then
                local fireDebounce = currentHum:FindFirstChild("FireDebounce")
                if fireDebounce then
                    getgenv().AntiBurnConn = fireDebounce.Changed:Connect(function()
                        if fireDebounce.Value == true then
                            local bar = Workspace.Plots.Plot1.Barrier.PlotBarrier
                            local pos = bar.CFrame
                            task.spawn(function()
                                repeat task.wait() bar.CFrame = plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame until not fireDebounce.Value
                            end)
                            task.wait(1)
                            fireDebounce.Value = false
                            task.wait()
                            bar.CFrame = pos
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end
        else
            if getgenv().AntiBurnConn then getgenv().AntiBurnConn:Disconnect() end
            if getgenv().AntiBurnCharConn then getgenv().AntiBurnCharConn:Disconnect() end
        end
    end
})

plr = Players.LocalPlayer
setOwner = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("GrabEvents"):WaitForChild("SetNetworkOwner")

function StartAntiBlobmanAura()
    while _G.AntiBlobmanAura do
        for _, player in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if player ~= plr and player.Character then
                playerRootPart = player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                playerHumanoid = player.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
                myCharacter = plr.Character
                myRootPart = myCharacter and myCharacter:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if player.Name ~= plr.Name 
                    and playerRootPart 
                    and myRootPart 
                    and playerHumanoid 
                    and playerHumanoid.SeatPart 
                    and playerHumanoid.SeatPart.Parent 
                    and playerHumanoid.SeatPart.Parent.Name == "CreatureBlobman" 
                    and (playerRootPart.Position - myRootPart.Position).Magnitude <= 19 then
                    pcall(function()
                        setOwner:FireServer(playerRootPart, playerRootPart.CFrame)
                    end)
                    task.wait(0.01)
                end
            end
        end
        task.wait(0.01)
    end
end

ClassicDefense:AddToggle("AntiBlobmanAura", {
    Text = "Anti Blobman Aura",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(v)
        _G.AntiBlobmanAura = v
        if v then
            task.spawn(StartAntiBlobmanAura)
        else
        end
    end
})

ClassicDefense:AddToggle("AntiBlobman", {
    Text = "Anti Blobman",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(v)
        if v then
            for i,v in pairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
                if v.Name == "CreatureBlobman" and not v:IsDescendantOf(inv) then 
                    rd, ld = v:FindFirstChild("RightDetector") or v:WaitForChild("RightDetector", 3), v:FindFirstChild("LeftDetector") or v:WaitForChild("LeftDetector", 3)
                    if rd and ld then 
                        rd.RightAlignOrientation.Enabled = false
                        rd.RightWeld.Enabled = false
                        ld.LeftAlignOrientation.Enabled = false
                        ld.LeftWeld.Enabled = false
                    end
                end
            end
            cons["antiblob"] = workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(d)
                if d.Name == "CreatureBlobman" and (not inv or not d:IsDescendantOf(inv)) then 
                    rd = d:FindFirstChild("RightDetector") or d:WaitForChild("RightDetector", 3)
                    ld = d:FindFirstChild("LeftDetector") or d:WaitForChild("LeftDetector", 3)
                    if rd and ld then
                        rao = rd:WaitForChild("RightAlignOrientation", 1)
                        rw  = rd:WaitForChild("RightWeld", 1)
                        lao = ld:WaitForChild("LeftAlignOrientation", 1)
                        lw  = ld:WaitForChild("LeftWeld", 1)
                        if rao then rao.Enabled = false end
                        if rw  then rw.Enabled  = false end
                        if lao then lao.Enabled = false end
                        if lw  then lw.Enabled  = false end
                    end
                end
            end)
        else
            if cons["antiblob"] then cons["antiblob"]:Disconnect() end
        end
    end
})

local antiRagBlobConnections = {}

ClassicDefense:AddToggle("AntiRagBlobToggle", {
    Text = "Anti Ragdoll on Blob",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(Value)
        antiRagBlobActive = Value
        RagdollRemote = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("RagdollRemote")
        RagdolledSit = false
        local function DiscAR(con)
            if antiRagBlobConnections[con] then
                antiRagBlobConnections[con]:Disconnect()
                antiRagBlobConnections[con] = nil
            end
        end
        local function setupCharacter(char)
            hum = char and char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
            HRP = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if hum and HRP and RagdollRemote then
                DiscAR("ARSeat")
                antiRagBlobConnections["ARSeat"] = hum:GetPropertyChangedSignal("SeatPart"):Connect(function()
                    if hum.SeatPart and hum.SeatPart.Parent and hum.SeatPart.Parent.Name == "CreatureBlobman" and not RagdolledSit then
                        RagdolledSit = true
                        Seat = hum.SeatPart
                        while not hum.Sit do task.wait() end
                        RagdollRemote:FireServer(HRP, 3)
                        while not (hum:FindFirstChild("Ragdolled") and hum.Ragdolled.Value) and not hum.Sit do task.wait() end
                        task.wait(0.4)
                        hum.Sit = false
                        if Seat and Seat:IsA("Part") then
                            Seat:Sit(hum)
                        end
                        task.delay(0.25, function()
                            while hum and hum.SeatPart do
                                if LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                                    RagdollRemote:FireServer(LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart, 1)
                                end
                                task.wait(0.05)
                            end
                            RagdolledSit = false
                        end)
                    end
                end)
            end
        end
        if antiRagBlobActive then
            setupCharacter(LocalPlayer.Character or LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Wait())
            DiscAR("ARChar")
            antiRagBlobConnections["ARChar"] = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(newChar)
                task.wait(0.5)
                setupCharacter(newChar)
            end)
        else
            for _, conn in pairs(antiRagBlobConnections) do
                if conn then conn:Disconnect() end
            end
            antiRagBlobConnections = {}
        end
    end
})

ClassicDefense:AddDivider()

ClassicDefense:AddLabel("Environment", true, Color3.fromRGB(100, 200, 100))

ClassicDefense:AddToggle("AntiExplode", {
    Text = "Anti Explode",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(v)
        if v then
            getgenv().AntiExplodeConn = Workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(function(c)
                if c.Name == "Part" then
                    char = plr.Character
                    if (c.Position - char.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude < 40 and char.Humanoid.Ragdolled.Value == true then
                        char.HumanoidRootPart.Anchored = true
                        task.wait(0.01)
                        char.HumanoidRootPart.Anchored = false
                        stvel(char.HumanoidRootPart)
                        char.Humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running)
                    end
                end
            end)
        else
            if getgenv().AntiExplodeConn then getgenv().AntiExplodeConn:Disconnect() end
        end
    end
})

    Text = "Anti Void",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(v)
        if v then
            Workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight = 0/0
        else
            Workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight = -100
        end
    end
})

local function getPing()
    local ping = game:GetService('Stats').Network.ServerStatsItem['Data Ping']:GetValue()
    return ping
end

GucciDefense = Tabs.DefenseTab:AddRightGroupbox("Gucci Methods")

GucciDefense:AddLabel("Invisibility Glitches", true, Color3.fromRGB(255, 215, 0))

GucciDefense:AddToggle("GucciTractor", {
    Text = "Gucci (Tractor)",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(v)
        _G.GucciTractorEnabled = v
        SaveToggleState("GucciTractor", v)
        if v then
            blobb = spawntoy("TractorGreen", plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(5, 5, 20))
            if not blobb then return end
            blobb.Name = "tractorgucci"
            blobb:WaitForChild("VehicleSeat", 3):Sit(plr.Character.Humanoid)
            task.spawn(function()
                endTime = tick() + 3
                while tick() < endTime do
                    functions.Ragdoll:FireServer(plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart, 0)
                    task.wait()
                end
            end)
            task.wait()
            while blobb.VehicleSeat.Occupant ~= plr.Character.Humanoid do task.wait() end
            plr.Character.Humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
            sno(blobb.Part)
            task.wait(0.2)
            blobb.VehicleSeat.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, 0/0, 0)
        else
            if inv:FindFirstChild("tractorgucci") then functions.DestroyToy:FireServer(inv.tractorgucci) end
            for i = 1, 30 do
                plr.Character.Humanoid.Sit = true
                task.wait()
                plr.Character.Humanoid.Sit = false
            end
        end
    end
})

local TrainGucciConn
local TrainGucciSeatMonitorConn
local TrainGucciCharConn
local TrainGucciDoing = false

local function ApplyTrainGucci()
    if TrainGucciDoing then return end
    TrainGucciDoing = true
    if TrainGucciConn then
        TrainGucciConn:Disconnect()
        TrainGucciConn = nil
    end
    if TrainGucciSeatMonitorConn then
        TrainGucciSeatMonitorConn:Disconnect()
        TrainGucciSeatMonitorConn = nil
    end
    local char = plr.Character
    local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then
        TrainGucciDoing = false
        return
    end
    local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not root then
        TrainGucciDoing = false
        return
    end
    local trainModel = workspace:FindFirstChild("Map")
        and workspace.Map:FindFirstChild("AlwaysHereTweenedObjects")
        and workspace.Map.AlwaysHereTweenedObjects:FindFirstChild("Train")
    if not trainModel then
        TrainGucciDoing = false
        return
    end
    local trainObj = trainModel:FindFirstChild("Object")
    if not trainObj then
        TrainGucciDoing = false
        return
    end
    local objModel = trainObj:FindFirstChild("ObjectModel")
    if not objModel then
        TrainGucciDoing = false
        return
    end
    local seat = objModel:FindFirstChild("Seat")
    if not seat then
        TrainGucciDoing = false
        return
    end
    local conn
    local completed = false
    pcall(function()
        conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if completed then return end
            local c = plr.Character
            local h = c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local r = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not (h and r and h.Health > 0) then
                completed = true
                if conn then conn:Disconnect() end
                TrainGucciDoing = false
                return
            end
            local loops = math.max(1, math.floor(getPing() / 10))
            for _ = 1, loops do
                if not _G.GucciTrainEnabled then
                    completed = true
                    if conn then conn:Disconnect() end
                    TrainGucciDoing = false
                    return
                end
                seat:Sit(h)
                RagdollRemote:FireServer(r, 0)
                task.wait()
            end
            local waitStart = tick()
            while h.Sit do
                if tick() - waitStart > 3 then break end
                if not _G.GucciTrainEnabled then
                    completed = true
                    if conn then conn:Disconnect() end
                    TrainGucciDoing = false
                    return
                end
                task.wait()
            end
            task.wait(0.09)
            completed = true
            if conn then conn:Disconnect() end
            TrainGucciConn = nil
            TrainGucciDoing = false
        end)
    end)
    TrainGucciConn = conn
end

GucciDefense:AddToggle("GucciTrainToggle", {
    Text = "Gucci (Train)",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(v)
        _G.GucciTrainEnabled = v
        SaveToggleState("GucciTrainToggle", v)
        if v then
            ApplyTrainGucci()
            if TrainGucciSeatMonitorConn then
                TrainGucciSeatMonitorConn:Disconnect()
                TrainGucciSeatMonitorConn = nil
            end
            local char = plr.Character
            local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if hum then
                local trainModel = workspace:FindFirstChild("Map")
                    and workspace.Map:FindFirstChild("AlwaysHereTweenedObjects")
                    and workspace.Map.AlwaysHereTweenedObjects:FindFirstChild("Train")
                if trainModel then
                    local trainObj = trainModel:FindFirstChild("Object")
                    if trainObj then
                        local objModel = trainObj:FindFirstChild("ObjectModel")
                        if objModel then
                            local seat = objModel:FindFirstChild("Seat")
                            if seat then
                                TrainGucciSeatMonitorConn = seat:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Occupant"):Connect(function()
                                    if not _G.GucciTrainEnabled then return end
                                    if TrainGucciDoing then return end
                                    local currentOccupant = seat.Occupant
                                    local myHum = plr.Character and plr.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                                    if myHum and currentOccupant and currentOccupant ~= myHum then
                                        task.wait(0.2)
                                        if _G.GucciTrainEnabled then
                                            ApplyTrainGucci()
                                        end
                                    end
                                end)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            if TrainGucciCharConn then
                TrainGucciCharConn:Disconnect()
                TrainGucciCharConn = nil
            end
            TrainGucciCharConn = plr.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(newChar)
                if not _G.GucciTrainEnabled then return end
                if TrainGucciDoing then return end
                task.wait(0.5)
                if _G.GucciTrainEnabled then
                    ApplyTrainGucci()
                end
            end)
        else
            TrainGucciDoing = false
            if TrainGucciConn then
                TrainGucciConn:Disconnect()
                TrainGucciConn = nil
            end
            if TrainGucciSeatMonitorConn then
                TrainGucciSeatMonitorConn:Disconnect()
                TrainGucciSeatMonitorConn = nil
            end
            if TrainGucciCharConn then
                TrainGucciCharConn:Disconnect()
                TrainGucciCharConn = nil
            end
            local char = plr.Character
            if char then
                local hum = char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
                if hum then
                    for i = 1, 30 do
                        hum.Sit = true
                        task.wait()
                        hum.Sit = false
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
})

GucciDefense:AddLabel("Blobman Glitches", true, Color3.fromRGB(255, 100, 255))

local spawnedInToysFolder = workspace:FindFirstChild(plr.Name.."SpawnedInToys") or workspace:WaitForChild(plr.Name.."SpawnedInToys", 5)
local DestroyToyEvent = Services.rs.MenuToys.DestroyToy
local SpawnToyRF = Services.rs.MenuToys.SpawnToyRemoteFunction
local IsHeld = plr:WaitForChild("IsHeld")
local SetNetworkOwner = Services.rs.GrabEvents.SetNetworkOwner
local RagdollRemote = Services.rs.CharacterEvents.RagdollRemote

workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
    if plr.Character and _G.BlobGucciEnabled then
        if obj.Parent == plr.Character and obj.Name == "LocalCreatureControl" then
            obj.Enabled = false
        end
    end
end)

local function doBlobGucci()
    for _, v in pairs(spawnedInToysFolder:GetChildren()) do
        if v.Name == "CreatureBlobman" or v.Name == "Gucci" then
            DestroyToyEvent:FireServer(v)
        end
    end
    SpawnToyRF:InvokeServer("CreatureBlobman", CFrame.new(0,2000,0), Vector3.new(0,60,0))
    local blob = spawnedInToysFolder:WaitForChild("CreatureBlobman", 5)
    if IsHeld.Value then
        repeat task.wait() until not IsHeld.Value
    end
    if blob then
        local seat = blob:WaitForChild("VehicleSeat")
        local bRoot = blob:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or blob.PrimaryPart
        local bHead = blob:WaitForChild("Head")
        local hitbox = blob:WaitForChild("GrabbableHitbox")
        local char = plr.Character or plr.CharacterAdded:Wait()
        local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or char:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local hum = char and char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") or char:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
        hum.Sit = true
        task.wait(0.02)
        hum.Sit = false
        task.wait(0.02)
        local safe = root.CFrame
        task.spawn(function()
            for _ = 1, 30 do
                if bHead:FindFirstChild("PartOwner") then
                    local vel = Instance.new("BodyVelocity", bRoot)
                    vel.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
                    vel.Velocity = Vector3.new(0,9e9,0)
                    break
                end
                root.CFrame = bRoot.CFrame
                task.wait(0.03)
                SetNetworkOwner:FireServer(hitbox, hitbox.CFrame)
                task.wait(0.03)
            end
        end)
        task.defer(function()
            local start = tick()
            while tick() - start < 0.3 do
                if seat.Occupant ~= hum then
                    seat:Sit(hum)
                end
                task.wait(0.03)
                hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
                task.wait(0.03)
            end
        end)
        local sitJump = true
        task.defer(function()
            while sitJump do
                RagdollRemote:FireServer(root, 0.095)
                task.wait(0.01)
            end
        end)
        task.wait(getPing() / 200)
        hum.Sit = false
        sitJump = false
        task.wait(getPing() / 800)
        root.CFrame = safe
        task.spawn(function()
            for _ = 1, 30 do
                task.wait()
                root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            end
        end)
        blob.Name = "Gucci"
        pcall(function()
            plr.PlayerGui.MenuGui.Menu.TabContents.ToyDestroy.Contents.CreatureBlobman.ViewItemButton.LowResImage:Destroy()
            plr.PlayerGui.MenuGui.Menu.TabContents.ToyDestroy.Contents.CreatureBlobman.ViewItemButton.Active = false
            plr.PlayerGui.MenuGui.Menu.TabContents.ToyDestroy.Contents.CreatureBlobman.ViewItemButton.Text = "GUCCI"
        end)
    end
end

local function UndoBlobGucci()
    for _, v in pairs(spawnedInToysFolder:GetChildren()) do
        if v.Name == "CreatureBlobman" or v.Name == "Gucci" then
            DestroyToyEvent:FireServer(v)
        end
    end
    task.wait(0.02)
    IsHeld.Value = true
    task.wait(0.02)
    IsHeld.Value = false
end

GucciDefense:AddToggle("GucciBlobman", {
    Text = "Auto Gucci (blobman)",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(Value)
        _G.BlobGucciEnabled = Value
        SaveToggleState("GucciBlobman", Value)
        if Value then
            doBlobGucci()
            if _G.GucciBlobmanMonitor then
                _G.GucciBlobmanMonitor:Disconnect()
                _G.GucciBlobmanMonitor = nil
            end
            _G.GucciBlobmanMonitor = game:GetService("RunService").Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                if not _G.BlobGucciEnabled then return end
                if _G.GucciBlobmanRedoing then return end
                local char = plr.Character
                local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if not hum then return end
                local folder = workspace:FindFirstChild(plr.Name .. "SpawnedInToys")
                if not folder then return end
                local gucciBlob = folder:FindFirstChild("Gucci")
                local needsRedo = false
                if not gucciBlob then
                    needsRedo = true
                else
                    local seat = gucciBlob:FindFirstChild("VehicleSeat")
                    if seat and seat.Occupant and seat.Occupant ~= hum then
                        needsRedo = true
                    end
                end
                if needsRedo then
                    _G.GucciBlobmanRedoing = true
                    task.spawn(function()
                        task.wait(0.5)
                        if _G.BlobGucciEnabled then
                            doBlobGucci()
                        end
                        task.wait(2)
                        _G.GucciBlobmanRedoing = false
                    end)
                end
            end)
            if _G.GucciBlobmanCharConn then
                _G.GucciBlobmanCharConn:Disconnect()
            end
            _G.GucciBlobmanCharConn = plr.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
                if _G.BlobGucciEnabled and not _G.GucciBlobmanRedoing then
                    _G.GucciBlobmanRedoing = true
                    task.spawn(function()
                        task.wait(0.5)
                        if _G.BlobGucciEnabled then
                            doBlobGucci()
                        end
                        task.wait(2)
                        _G.GucciBlobmanRedoing = false
                    end)
                end
            end)
        else
            UndoBlobGucci()
            _G.GucciBlobmanRedoing = false
            if _G.GucciBlobmanMonitor then
                _G.GucciBlobmanMonitor:Disconnect()
                _G.GucciBlobmanMonitor = nil
            end
            if _G.GucciBlobmanCharConn then
                _G.GucciBlobmanCharConn:Disconnect()
                _G.GucciBlobmanCharConn = nil
            end
        end
    end
})

hkSalmonList = {}

hkSalmonList = {}
hkExpectDeath = false

local function hkApplySalmon(char)
    if not char then
        return
    end
    newHum = char:WaitForChild('Humanoid', 5)
    if not newHum then
        return
    end
    if hkSalmonList[player.UserId] and not hkExpectDeath then
        hkExpectDeath = true
        grabParts = workspace:FindFirstChild('GrabParts')
        savedGrab = grabParts and grabParts:Clone()
        newHum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead)
        if savedGrab then
            task.delay(0.1, function()
                if not workspace:FindFirstChild('GrabParts') and savedGrab and savedGrab.Parent == nil then
                    savedGrab.Parent = workspace
                end
            end)
        end
    else
        hkExpectDeath = false
    end
end

ExtraDefense:AddToggle("HKAntiKick", {
    Text = "Auto Break PCLD [Reset]",
    Default = true,
    Callback = function(Value)
        hkSalmonList[player.UserId] = Value and true or nil
        if Value and player.Character and not workspace:FindFirstChild('GrabParts') then
            task.spawn(function()
                hkApplySalmon(player.Character)
            end)
        end
    end
})

if player.Character then
    task.spawn(function()
        if hkSalmonList[player.UserId] and not workspace:FindFirstChild('GrabParts') then
            hkApplySalmon(player.Character)
        end
    end)
end

player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
    if hkSalmonList[player.UserId] then
        hkApplySalmon(char)
    end
end)

    Text = "Anti Ownership (freeze)",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(v)
        getgenv().AntiOwnershipActive = v
        if v then
            task.spawn(function()
                Struggle = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):WaitForChild("CharacterEvents"):WaitForChild("Struggle")
                while getgenv().AntiOwnershipActive do
                    char = plr.Character
                    if char and char:FindFirstChild("Head") then
                        head = char.Head
                        if head:FindFirstChild("PartOwner") then
                            Struggle:FireServer(plr)
                            for _, part in pairs(char:GetChildren()) do
                                if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                                    part.Anchored = true
                                end
                            end
                            isHeld = plr:FindFirstChild("IsHeld")
                            while isHeld and isHeld.Value and getgenv().AntiOwnershipActive do
                                task.wait()
                            end
                            for _, part in pairs(char:GetChildren()) do
                                if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                                    part.Anchored = false
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    task.wait(0.1)
                end
            end)
        else
            char = plr.Character
            if char then
                for _, part in pairs(char:GetChildren()) do
                    if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                        part.Anchored = false
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
})

ExtraDefense:AddToggle("UFOShurikenStick", {
    Text = "break UFO 1 (shuriken)",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(state)
        if not state then return end
        Players = game:GetService("Players")
        RS = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
        LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
        PlayerName = LocalPlayer.Name
        StickyEvent = RS:WaitForChild("PlayerEvents"):WaitForChild("StickyPartEvent")
        SpawnRemote = RS.MenuToys:WaitForChild("SpawnToyRemoteFunction")
        CanSpawn = LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("CanSpawnToy")
        ToysFolder = workspace:WaitForChild(PlayerName .. "SpawnedInToys")
        UFOs = {
            workspace.Map.AlwaysHereTweenedObjects:FindFirstChild("InnerUFO"),
            workspace.Map.AlwaysHereTweenedObjects:FindFirstChild("OuterUFO")
        }
        local function getHRP()
            if LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                return LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart
            end
            return LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Wait():WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        end
        task.spawn(function()
            for i = 1, 12 do
                t = tick()
                while not CanSpawn.Value do
                    if tick() - t > 5 then break end
                    task.wait(0.1)
                end
                hrp = getHRP()
                if hrp then
                    pcall(function()
                        SpawnRemote:InvokeServer("NinjaShuriken", hrp.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 10, 15), Vector3.new())
                    end)
                end
                task.wait(0.15)
            end
            task.wait(1)
            for _, Toy in ipairs(ToysFolder:GetChildren()) do
                if Toy.Name == "NinjaShuriken" and Toy:FindFirstChild("StickyPart") then
                    for _, UFO in ipairs(UFOs) do
                        if UFO and UFO:FindFirstChild("Object") and UFO.Object:FindFirstChild("ObjectModel") and UFO.Object.ObjectModel:FindFirstChild("Body") then
                            StickyEvent:FireServer(Toy.StickyPart, UFO.Object.ObjectModel.Body, CFrame.new())
                            follow = UFO.Object:FindFirstChild("FollowThisPart")
                            if follow then
                                if follow:FindFirstChild("AlignOrientation") then follow.AlignOrientation.Enabled = false end
                                if follow:FindFirstChild("AlignPosition") then follow.AlignPosition.Enabled = false end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
    end
})
